- **transcription-factor-cofactor-motif-annotation**: Use when you have DESSO motifs/TFBSs and ENCODE ChIP-seq peaks for multiple TFs in one cell line and need to classify indirect binding and infer TF-cofactor tethering from motif co-occurrence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-cofactor-motif-annotation))
- **transcription-factor-composite-ranking**: Use when DC3 has assigned scRNA-seq and scATAC-seq cells to subpopulations and you need to rank transcription factors driving each subpopulation by joint expression and motif enrichment evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-composite-ranking))
- **transcription-factor-cooperative-binding-detection**: Use when you have a chromVARDeviations object with precomputed bias-corrected deviations and z-scores for multiple annotation sets (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/transcription-factor-cooperative-binding-detection))
- **transcription-factor-database-selection**: Use when running TFvelo on scRNA-seq data and you need to restrict the TF pool per target gene — e.g., preparing the gene regulatory input before weight initialization and velocity inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-database-selection))
- **transcription-factor-footprint-scoring**: Use when after bias-corrected ATAC-seq signal tracks (bigWig files) have been generated and you need to quantify transcription factor binding strength within open chromatin regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/transcription-factor-footprint-scoring))
- **transcription-factor-hit-prioritization**: Use when you have a fitted PRESCIENT model (data_pt) on time-series scRNA-seq and need to identify which transcription factors significantly bias differentiation toward a target fate before designing an in vitro perturbational screen. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-hit-prioritization))
- **transcription-factor-marker-prioritization**: Use when scRNAseq clusters have been defined (e.g., via rCASC clustering) and the goal is to identify transcription factors whose target-derived latent representation drives cluster aggregation, rather than generic differential expression markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-marker-prioritization))
- **transcription-factor-motif-database-matching**: Use when a set of de novo TF sequence motifs (e.g., predicted by DESSO from ENCODE ChIP-seq peaks) needs to be labeled as matching documented JASPAR/TRANSFAC motifs or flagged as novel/undocumented. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-motif-database-matching))
- **transcription-factor-motif-enrichment**: Use when a Mowgli factor's H(atac) matrix rows must be biologically annotated: top-weighted peaks per factor are tested for TF motif over-representation to name the factor's putative regulator. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-motif-enrichment))
- **transcription-factor-motif-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have binarized topic-enriched regions from a cisTopic model on single-cell ATAC-seq data and want to recover topic-specific TF cistromes and master regulators such as SPI1 and CEBPB. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-motif-enrichment-analysis))
- **transcription-factor-motif-enrichment-interpretation**: Use when assigning functional titles or annotations to BloodGen3 (or similar fixed) module gene lists, and transcription-factor motif enrichment (e.g. RcisTarget) is needed as convergent evidence alongside ontology/pathway enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-motif-enrichment-interpretation))
- **transcription-factor-motif-genomic-coordinate-annotation**: Use when you have ATAC-seq BAM files and want to detect transcription factor footprints—characteristic depletion patterns of Tn5 insertions around protein-bound motif sites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/transcription-factor-motif-genomic-coordinate-annotation))
- **transcription-factor-motif-matching**: Use when you have corrected ATAC-seq footprint scores (from ATACorrect and ScoreBigwig) at open chromatin regions and a motif database (e.g., JASPAR PWMs), and you need to determine which TF motif matches are actually occupied across one or more experimental conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/transcription-factor-motif-matching))
- **transcription-factor-motif-occurrence-analysis**: Use when the input is a genome-wide TF motif occurrence track (e.g. JASPAR PWM-derived BED with strand annotations) and the goal is to quantify orientation bias — same vs opposite strand — between consecutive or paired motif occurrences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-motif-occurrence-analysis))
- **transcription-factor-motif-scanning**: Use when you have FASTA sequences under predicted ChIP-enriched peaks (plus matched control sequences) and need to verify enrichment of an expected TF binding motif family, or compare motif enrichment across peak ranks or platforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-motif-scanning))
- **transcription-factor-network-analysis**: Use when a transcriptomics (or phospho-proteomic) dataset must be summarized into per-regulator activity scores and the user runs multiple inference methods simultaneously and needs one consensus estimate per regulator. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-network-analysis))
- **transcription-factor-network-inference**: Use when a CellexalVR in-session cell group needs a TF-only co-expression network: the input is expression values for the selected cells and the goal is a tf_network.txt consumed by the VR TF network generator. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-network-inference))
- **transcription-factor-panel-verification**: Use when a TF-based SCA latent-space frequency matrix plus cluster assignments (e.g., setA clusters 1 and 2) are available and the goal is to extract and verify an optimal gene marker panel (≤4 genes, negations allowed) per cluster with COMET. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-panel-verification))
- **transcription-factor-pathway-interaction-analysis**: Use when analyzing time-series gene expression profiles (e.g., RNA-seq across a biological process such as liver regeneration) to identify which TFs and pathways are activated or repressed at specific time points. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-pathway-interaction-analysis))
- **transcription-factor-peak-calling**: Use when ChIP-seq FASTQ data for a DNA-binding factor (e.g., LuxR or H-NS in V. harveyi) needs trimming, reference alignment, and macs2 peak calling to count and compare binding peaks across conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-peak-calling))
- **transcription-factor-perturbation-analysis**: Use when you have a trained PRESCIENT model on time-series scRNA-seq data and need to test whether perturbing candidate TFs (e.g. neutrophil or monocyte regulators) shifts simulated cells toward or away from expected terminal fates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-perturbation-analysis))
- **transcription-factor-rank-benchmarking**: Use when comparing TR-identification tools on query region sets where the true perturbed/active TR is known, and a rank-based metric (MRR) over ranked TR output is needed for fair method comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-rank-benchmarking))
- **transcription-factor-regulatory-network-simulation**: Use when you need synthetic single-cell expression count matrices with known branched trajectory trees — e.g. to benchmark trajectory or tree-alignment methods where ground-truth topology and pseudotime are required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-regulatory-network-simulation))
- **transcription-factor-regulatory-potential-computation**: Use when you have four single-cell datasets (one scRNA-seq + one scATAC-seq from each of two populations) with consistent cluster assignments, and you need to detect subpopulation-specific differential TF-target regulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-regulatory-potential-computation))
- **transcription-factor-target-enrichment-analysis**: Use when evaluating whether inferred gene regulatory network edges are biologically meaningful — e.g., testing if a TF's neighbors in a cell-type-specific network match its ENCODE-validated targets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-target-enrichment-analysis--0f53fff212970a26))
- **transcription-factor-target-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a list of predicted circRNA/RBP candidate regulators and a gold-standard regulator list, and need to test shared transcription factor modulation (or related Harmonizome associations) to validate the candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-target-enrichment-analysis--0fc3b562f1e2c623))
- **transcription-factor-target-gene-identification**: Use when you have a TF ChIP-seq peak file (e.g. an ENCODE BED file) and need the set of unique genes whose associated REMs overlap the TF's binding regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-target-gene-identification))
- **transcription-factor-target-identification**: Use when the goal is to identify candidate transcription factor targets by correlating gene expression across many diverse GEO experiments, given a mean-centered COnTORT output file with GenBank annotation columns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-target-identification))
- **transcription-factor-weight-bounded-regression**: Use when modeling a target gene's RNA dynamics as a weighted function of candidate TF expressions (from ENCODE/ChEA) and you need bounded, solvable TF weight estimates during EM-based gene dynamics fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-weight-bounded-regression))
- **transcription-factor-weight-ranking**: Use when TFvelo has been run on a scRNA-seq dataset (rc.h5ad with TF weight matrix) and you need to identify the top-weighted regulators or verify TF weight signs on pathway genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcription-factor-weight-ranking))
- **transcription-fidelity**: Compare a rendered source page with its TeX transcription and blueprint anchor, catching signs, indices, relation symbols, omitted clauses, and page-coordinate errors before formalization relies on them. ([YijunYuan/homalg-horizon](https://github.com/YijunYuan/homalg-horizon/tree/HEAD/src/archon_horizon/skills/transcription-fidelity))
- **transcription-helper**: Guides users through video transcription workflow from input to output. Transcribes local video files and YouTube URLs using gpt-4o-transcribe. Use when users want to transcribe videos, audio files, YouTube content, or need help with media-to-text conversion. ([costiash/CognivAgent](https://github.com/costiash/CognivAgent/tree/HEAD/app/agent/resources/.claude/skills/transcription-helper) · ★4)
- **transcription-initiation-signal-regression**: Use when benchmarking 100,000-bp DNA sequence models on nucleotide-wise signal-intensity regression for transcription initiation, or when verifying that DNA foundation models underperform a task-specific expert model on TISP. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcription-initiation-signal-regression))
- **transcription-model-research**: Find speech-to-text models for transcription or dictation on Hugging Face by searching every task where they live, not just ASR. Dedicated ASR models are under automatic-speech-recognition, audio-language models that transcribe when prompted are under audio-text-to-text, and omni models under any-to-any. Filter by language, size, streaming, timestamps and on-device formats, then shortlist. Use on "find a transcription model", "best dictation model for X", "speech to text for Hebrew/Spanish/…", "Whisper alternatives", "on-device ASR", "model for voice typing", "subtitles model". ([danielrosehill/AI-Model-Research-Plugin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/AI-Model-Research-Plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/transcription-model-research))
- **transcription-pro**: Master transcription — speech-to-text workflows, accuracy optimization, speaker diarization, and transcript management. ([aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills](https://github.com/aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/transcription-pro) · ★68 · 2 copies)
- **transcription-reader**: Efficiently read, parse, and analyze transcription and subtitle files (STJ, VTT, SRT, ASS, SSA). Use this skill whenever the user uploads or references a transcription file, subtitle file, or meeting recording transcript. Trigger on file extensions like .stj, .stjson, .vtt, .srt, .ass, .ssa, or when the user mentions transcripts, captions, subtitles, meeting notes from recordings, or wants to summarize a recording, find who said what, extract action items, or convert between transcription formats. ([yaniv-golan/skill-packager-skill](https://github.com/yaniv-golan/skill-packager-skill/tree/HEAD/skill-packager-workspace/iteration-1/eval-2-zip-only/without_skill/outputs/extracted/baseline-transcription-reader) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **transcription-skill**: Transcribe speech in audio or video files (mp4, mov, mkv, wav, m4a, mp3) into a structured, timestamped Transcript with a local ASR engine (faster-whisper), Japanese and English first-class; export plain SRT/VTT; derive speech intervals. Use this skill whenever the user asks what is said in a recording, wants a transcript, timestamps of speech, word timings, or timed text to hand to a subtitle or editing tool. It does not edit video, style subtitles, detect speakers, or decide anything about a production. ([kajisho5/transcription-skill](https://github.com/kajisho5/transcription-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **transcription-start-site-validation**: Use when a long-read-derived isoform catalog (e.g. lrCaptureSeq GFF) needs independent 5'-end validation and matched-tissue CAGE-seq data (e.g. adult mouse retina, DDBJ DRA002410) is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/transcription-start-site-validation))
- **transcription-subtitles**: Transcribe audio to text with speaker diarization and export as SRT/VTT subtitles. Use when a user asks to "transcribe this", "make subtitles", "add captions", or "get speaker labels". ([simply-ehis/otoyin](https://github.com/simply-ehis/otoyin/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/transcription-subtitles))
- **transcription-to-content**: Transform podcast transcripts into multiple content assets—blog posts, social snippets, newsletters, and SEO-optimized landing pages—using systematic repurposing workflows. Use when: Maximizing ROI from podcast episodes; Creating blog content from audio/video; Generating social media posts from long-form content; Building newsletter content from transcripts; Extracting quotes and highlights for promotion ([NVlabs/Skill2Env](https://github.com/NVlabs/Skill2Env/tree/HEAD/SkillHub/skills/transcription-to-content) · ★212 · 4 copies)
- **transcription-translation**: Transcribe DNA to RNA and translate to protein using Biopython. Use when converting between DNA, RNA, and protein sequences, finding ORFs, or using alternative codon tables. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/sequence-manipulation-transcription-translation) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **transcription-translation**: Transcribe DNA to RNA (and back), translate coding sequences to protein using NCBI codon tables, and pick the right genetic code for mitochondria, plastids, or non-standard organisms. ([pradyumnasagar/open-research-skills](https://github.com/pradyumnasagar/open-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bioinformatics-sequence/transcription-translation) · ★8)
- **transcription-translation**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Sequence_Analysis/sequence-manipulation/transcription-translation) · ★7)
- **transcription-vocabulary-suggest**: Intelligently suggest and add custom vocabulary for speech transcription by analyzing Chief-of-Staff README, space context, and key memory files. ([mindstone/rebel-system](https://github.com/mindstone/rebel-system/tree/HEAD/skills/system/transcription-vocabulary-suggest))
- **transcription-voice-system**: Guide for working on the Transcription, Voice, and Wake Word system spanning Rust (Tauri), Python (FastAPI), and React layers. Use when editing Voice.tsx, use-transcription.ts, use-wake-word.ts, use-transcription-sessions.ts, TranscriptionContext, WakeWordContext, wake_word_routes, transcription/ Rust module, session persistence, or any audio/whisper/wake-word related code. Also use when adding new AI tools that interact with voice/transcription data. ([armanisadeghi/matrx-local](https://github.com/armanisadeghi/matrx-local/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/transcription-voice-system))
- **transcription-workflow**: Lessons learned from the Stage 1.4 transcription phase (~140 PRs, 235 pages, 5+ concurrent agents). ([mathlib-initiative/Etingof-RepresentationTheory-draft1](https://github.com/mathlib-initiative/Etingof-RepresentationTheory-draft1/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/transcription-workflow) · ★2)
- **transcriptional-bursting-parameter-interpretation**: Use when raw single-cell count matrix needs gene-level kon/koff/s kinetic parameter estimates (e.g., to parameterize SymSim's Beta-Poisson model, or to characterize transcriptional bursting per cell cluster). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptional-bursting-parameter-interpretation))
- **transcriptional-module-co-clustering**: Use when a fixed module repertoire needs a two-tier organization — grouping modules into aggregates based on similarity of activity profiles across the reference datasets that were used to build the repertoire. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptional-module-co-clustering))
- **transcriptional-network-master-regulator-analysis**: Use when you need to infer which transcription factors drive temporal gene programs in scRNA-seq trajectories (e.g. blastocyst EPI/PE lineages) so ligand-receptor pairs can be causally linked to TF activity downstream. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptional-network-master-regulator-analysis))
- **transcriptional-regulator-ranking-from-bit-scores**: Use when you have a processed peak/DAR region set (BED with chromosome coordinates, e.g., from DiffBind or MACS2) and need to identify and rank candidate transcriptional regulators with uncertainty estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcriptional-regulator-ranking-from-bit-scores))
- **transcriptional-regulatory-network-inference**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq analysis needs cell type-specific TF-TF/TF-TG regulatory networks and ranked key regulators (driving forces) after cell types and signatures have been defined. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptional-regulatory-network-inference))
- **transcriptional-regulatory-network-inference-from-scrnaseq**: Use when you have a time-course or trajectory-ordered scRNA-seq dataset with cell-type labels and need to identify upstream temporal transcription factors (e.g., as downstream targets of LR signaling) via TRN inference plus MRA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptional-regulatory-network-inference-from-scrnaseq))
- **transcriptional-strand-asymmetry-estimation**: Use when strand-annotated motifs (BED files with '+'/'-' strands) must be tested for same-vs-opposite orientation bias relative to template/non-template direction of strand-annotated regions such as GENCODE protein-coding genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/transcriptional-strand-asymmetry-estimation))
- **transcriptional-strand-bias-testing**: Use when a mutational signature call from fitting (mmsig, deconstructSigs, MutationalPatterns) needs independent confirmation — e.g., platinum signature SBS35 in therapy-related AML — and WGS data with strand annotations are available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptional-strand-bias-testing))
- **transcriptional-subtype-classification-transfer**: Use when a new bulk RNA-Seq cohort needs subtype labels propagated from an already-annotated reference cohort, especially when cohorts share no samples and only partially overlapping genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/transcriptional-subtype-classification-transfer))
- **transcriptional-time-lag-edge-orientation**: Use when you have a time-series RNA-Seq count table (e.g., DREAM 4 100-gene/21-sample, C. beijerinckii 6 time points) and need to assign direction (TF→TG) and sign (activation/repression) to mutual-information-derived edges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptional-time-lag-edge-orientation))
- **transcriptlab**: Use when the user wants a YouTube transcript, a transcript of an audio or video file, caption languages for a video, a search inside a transcript, their TranscriptLab credit balance, or to revisit a transcript they pulled before. ([hussamsi2040/transcriptlab-claude-plugin](https://github.com/hussamsi2040/transcriptlab-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/transcriptlab))
- **transcriptome-3-prime-truncation**: Use when building a custom pseudoalignment target for 3'-end tag scRNA-seq data (e.g., 10X Chromium) where reads do not span cleavage sites and full-length transcripts would collapse 3'UTR isoforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptome-3-prime-truncation))
- **transcriptome-alignment-tsv-parsing**: Use when raw Nanopore DRS reads must be aligned to a reference transcriptome (not a genome) and converted into a per-base alignment TSV that RedNano's extract_features.py can consume as basecalling-error input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/transcriptome-alignment-tsv-parsing))
- **transcriptome-assembly-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when comparing transcriptome assembler output on the same RNA-seq reads: you have multiple GTF assemblies (e.g., from single-aligner runs and merged-alignment runs) and need correct-transcript counts, precision, and F-score against a known reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptome-assembly-benchmark-evaluation))
- **transcriptome-assembly-filtering**: Use when you have a de novo assembled transcriptome (e.g. Trinity output) plus the original RNA-Seq reads and need to remove low-expression, redundant, and chimeric contigs without a reference genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptome-assembly-filtering))
- **transcriptome-assembly-parameter-tradeoff-analysis**: Use when configuring a de novo transcriptome assembler (rnaSPAdes, SPAdes, Trinity, RNA-Bloom, Trans-ABySS) for RNA-Seq reads of known length and you must decide between automatic vs. manually specified k-mer iterations, or when explaining assembly completeness-vs-accuracy differences attributable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/transcriptome-assembly-parameter-tradeoff-analysis))
- **transcriptome-assembly-quality-benchmarking**: Use when multiple transcriptome assemblies built from the same reads (long-read-only, short-read-only, hybrid merged BAM) must be ranked by Ex90N50 and BUSCO completeness to decide which assembly strategy to carry into annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/transcriptome-assembly-quality-benchmarking))
- **transcriptome-chimera-filtering-pipeline-execution**: Use when a de novo assembled transcriptome (e.g. Trinity contigs from FASTQ reads) needs chimera removal and quality improvement before downstream gene discovery or differential expression analyses. - **transcriptome-chimera-identification-blastx**: Use when you have a de novo assembled transcriptome (e.g. Trinity FASTA) with no reference genome and need to count or mark chimeric transcripts to assess or benchmark assembly filtering. - **transcriptome-clustering-comparison**: Use when you need to quantify whether parallelizing RCC's consensus clustering across multiple CCP processes changes clustering accuracy (ARI) or processing time relative to a single-process run with more repeats. - **transcriptome-evolutionary-index-calculation**: Use when single-cell RNA data (Scanpy AnnData) needs a gene age-weighted expression index per cell, stage, or cell type — e.g. to detect whether young gene age classes peak in early embryos — or when per-class partial TEI / relative expression profiles are required. - **transcriptome-metabolome-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have paired spatial transcriptome and metabolome feature matrices (e.g., from . - **transcriptome-quality-assessment**: Use when you have only sequencing reads and a de novo transcriptome assembly (no reference genome) and need a baseline assembly-quality score before filtering chimeric or low-evidence contigs. - **transcriptome-quantitation-with-rsem**: Use when sample-specific transcriptomes (e.g. aptardi or StringTie/Ensembl GTFs) need per-transcript expression counts for filtering or differential expression analysis. - **transcriptome-read-simulation**: Use when a de novo transcriptome pipeline (e.g., chimera removal) must be benchmarked against a known ground truth: a reference transcriptome and expression profile are available to simulate reads from. - **transcriptome-reconstruction-from-rnaseq**: Use when you have genome-aligned (or alignable) RNA-Seq reads and need a sample-specific GTF transcriptome with accurate 3'-ends/polyA sites for downstream quantitation or APA analysis. - **transcriptome-reconstruction-stringtie**: Use when you have a sorted BAM of genome-aligned RNA-Seq reads (e.g. HISAT2 --dta output) and need an initial GTF transcriptome as input for aptardi polyA site prediction or sample-specific 3'-end refinement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptome-reconstruction-stringtie))
- **transcriptome-reference-alignment**: Use when long-read single-cell cDNA or amplicon reads (ONT or PacBio) must be mapped to a reference transcriptome to call isoforms, fusions, SNVs, or per-gene/per-cell molecule counts. - **transcriptome-region-overlap-counting**: Use when counting overlaps between transcriptome region sets or between isoform-ambiguous genomic features and transcriptome regions of interest (ROI), and when RgnTX's overlapCountsTx / overlapCountsTxIA measures must be reproduced or verified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptome-region-overlap-counting))
- **transcriptome-region-randomization**: Use when you need null/randomized region sets that respect transcriptome annotation (e.g., for permutation tests of RNA element colocalization) or want to benchmark shuffling runtime over region-count grids. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptome-region-randomization))
- **transcriptome-to-genome-coordinate-liftover**: Use when m6A calls are reported in transcript coordinates (e.g. per-transcript positions from Nanom6A, Yanocomp, DRUMMER, EpiNano) but downstream benchmarking requires genomic positions comparable to a gold-standard peaks BED file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/transcriptome-to-genome-coordinate-liftover))
- **transcriptome-versus-genome-reference-prediction**: Use when benchmarking phasiRNA prediction tools' runtime on the same sRNA library (e.g., rice SRR5049781) across cDNA and gDNA references, or when deciding whether multithreading (e.g., -j 4) is needed to make genome-based prediction tractable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/transcriptome-versus-genome-reference-prediction))
- **transcriptome-wide-pseudouridine-prediction**: Use when you need transcriptome-wide, base-resolution prediction of novel RNA pseudouridine sites in human protein-coding genes using a trained sequence-plus-secondary-structure deep learning model. - **transcriptome-wide-similarity-search**: Use when you have a single-cell expression matrix for one study (e.g. H5AD-imported study in Cellenium) and want to find, for a query gene, all genes with correlated expression across cells. - **transfer-rumor-check**: 查证足球转会传闻的可信度。当用户提到一个转会说法——哪怕只是模糊一句"听说X要去Y队""刷到Z要转会了""这个转会靠谱吗"——想知道靠不靠谱/是真是假/查证一下时使用。沿两个正交维度判断——真实性（这个转会说法有多可能是真的，六档）+ 时效性（它现在还成不成立，含联网核实转会最新状态）——分开呈现，绝不合成一个分。核心方法：**信源是系统主动去联网检索的，不是等用户提供**——用户给的（球员+球队+方向）是检索线索，skill 拿它去查"谁在报这事、最可靠的源说到哪一步"，用检索到的报道场判可信度。信源当"证据"用（用"谁说的"推断"有多可能真"），不给媒体本身打分/做画像。不要触发：只是聊某笔转会的看法、问球员数据/身价、讨论战术——那些不是查证传闻。仅处理纯文字输入（不接链接抓取/截图识图）。国际传闻走机构为主的分级；中超/中甲/中国球员传闻走另一套中文一手源分级。 ([Wolinaxia/transfer-rumor-check](https://github.com/Wolinaxia/transfer-rumor-check/tree/HEAD/claude/transfer-rumor-check) · ★7)
